## BLOCKER

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defect hunt on demo/color-picke` · C-1 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** A cold load / refresh / shared link on any route whose left pane is not the color-picker (10 of 14 routes) permanently stalls the overture beat DAG at B2: overture:b3 and overture:b4 never fire for the life of the session, and the picker's hero-blob ornament (v-if gated on b4) never mounts again on ANY route thereafter.

**Mechanism.** State derived from a source of truth that is not yet true: index.html:205-213 mounts without awaiting router.isReady(), so the first render uses the picker config; PaneSlot then swaps the child (router resolution + the deliberate one-rAF commit at PaneSlot.vue:95), which cancels Vue's `appear` transition so @after-appear never fires. B3 is the one beat in this boot chain with NO state-checked fallback (contrast useDockArrival.ts:63-72 and useOverture.ts:173), so one missed DOM event is terminal.

**Evidence.**

```
Measured with an isolated Chromium probe against localhost:9000. CONTROL cold-load /#/ → marks [b0,b1,b3,b2,b4], `.hero-blob-anchor` present. DEEPLINK /#/generate → marks [b0,b1,b2] only, blob absent; after hash-nav to /#/ still [b0,b1,b2], `.picker-shell` present but blob STILL absent. Identical for /#/browse. First-pane oracle (pre-navigation MutationObserver) on cold /#/generate records `picker-shell` as the FIRST mounted left pane before the GeneratePane. Code: App.vue:90 and App.vue:109 are the ONLY call sites of overture.noteLeftPlateSettled (grep), both via PaneSlot.vue:118 `@after-appear`; useViewManager.ts:43-46 falls back to "picker" while route.name is undefined; PaneSlot.vue:74 seeds liveComponent at setup; useOverture.ts:153-183 gates b4 on b3Complete.
```

**Reproduction.** node <scratchpad>/probe-b3.mjs — launches chromium 1440x900, goto http://localhost:9000/#/generate (or /#/browse), wait 5s, read performance marks + `.hero-blob-anchor`; then set location.hash='#/', wait 7s, re-read. Compare against goto('/#/'). Deep links yield 3 marks and no blob; the control yields 5 marks and a blob.

**Proposed cure.** Take the MT-F012 entry cure and make it the idiomatic vue-router SPA boot: in the new main.ts do `app.use(router); await router.isReady(); app.mount('#app')`. With the route true at first render the first-mounted left pane is the final one, its appear completes, B3 and B4 fire. Second, independent arm: arm B3 on the left pane's MOUNT plus a getAnimations() settle check (the sibling beats' idiom) instead of a single Transition appear event.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** No entry module exists; App.vue is the composition root by default and silently carries the whole boot contract (router-plugin dependency, fouc-guard lockstep, body mount, CSS document root, two app-level installs in a component setup).

**Mechanism.** missing composition root — boot logic migrates into the root component because HTML cannot express one

**Evidence.**

```
`find demo -name "main.ts" -o -name "main.js"` → (no output). Entry = demo/color-picker/index.html:205-213 (inline `<script type="module">`). Assumption (a): App.vue:274 `useViewManager()` → demo/shell/useViewManager.ts:36-37 `useRouter()`/`useRoute()` then :41 `router.isReady()`. (b): index.html:159-201 fouc-guard; ground.ts:40-46 "LOCKSTEP CLAUSE 2" records the predicate having drifted (dropped 'auto' arm). (d): App.vue:199-210 four CSS imports; demo/styles/foundation.css:1,2,56,57,76,81,85 is itself the barrel. (e): App.vue:214 `useGlobalDark()`, :219 `provideApiClient()`, rationale at :212-213 = a mount-order contract in a comment. Build-side proof already measured in MT-F012.1: 698-byte application-free entry chunk, content hash `Dezn_h7o` byte-identical to a stock-Vite inline-entry repro.
```

**Reproduction.** docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/probes/vite-entry-repro/ for the build half. Structural half: remove `app.use(router)` from index.html:211 → TypeError at useViewManager.ts:41, a dependency App.vue never declares.

**Proposed cure.** Create demo/color-picker/main.ts holding createApp + `app.use(router)` + `app.provide(API_CLIENT_KEY, createApiClient())` + `initGlobalDark()` + `import "../styles/foundation.css"`, referenced as `<script type="module" src="./main.ts">`. Assumptions (a), (d), (e) become explicit compiler-checked lines; (c) becomes expressible; (b) reduces to the irreducible pre-module guard. App.vue drops both installs and all four CSS imports and becomes pure layout. Also fixes MT-F012 as a side effect — this is the form Vite documents.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The color-space catalog has four independent homes and no total consumer. PICKER_SPACE_NAMES→DISPLAY_COLOR_SPACE_NAMES = 18 members (this component's catalog); colorSpaceInfo = 13; MarkdownSpace = 11; INTERPOLATION_SPACES = 9. Seven of the 18 offered rows have no markdown doc; five have no info row. Every consumer covers the shortfall with a masking fallback (`: colorSpaceInfo.rgb`), an `as any`, or a silent `v-if`.

**Mechanism.** absent unique semantic ownership — one concept (the space catalog) split across four modules with zero type-level relation, so partial consumers mask the gap instead of failing to compile

**Evidence.**

```
demo/color-session/picker-color.ts:72 (17 labels) → demo/color-session/color-model.ts:75 (+hex = 18) → ColorSpaceSelector.vue:150. demo/color-session/colorSpaceInfo.ts:18 (13 keys). demo/scenes/about/AboutPane.vue:82 (MarkdownSpace, 11). demo/color-session/color-space-meta.ts:26 (9). Fallbacks: demo/scenes/about/ColorNutritionLabel.vue:212-214 `space in colorSpaceInfo ? … : colorSpaceInfo.rgb`; demo/picker/controls/ComponentSliders/ConsoleRail.vue:174 `(colorSpaceInfo as any)[space]`; demo/scenes/about/AboutPane.vue:51 `v-if="activeMarkdownModule"`. Live probe output: ABOUT AFTER display-p3 { "nutrition": "Definition | A color space based on the additive mixture of red, green, and blue light. | … | White Point: | Varies (typically D65) | Gamut: | Limited (device-specific) | Created: | 1931 | Components | Red | 0 ", "guide": "Detailed Guide", "triggers": ["Display P3","Display P3"] }. Screenshot: docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/ColorSpaceSelector/probe-display-p3-about.png
```

**Reproduction.** Dev server at http://localhost:9000/#/ (1600x1000). Click the color-space title in the picker card, select "Display P3". Both titles read "Display P3"; the About card beneath reads CIE RGB facts (Created: 1931, Components Red/Green/Blue) and the "Detailed Guide" heading has nothing under it. Script: scratchpad/chL-css-probe.mjs

**Proposed cure.** One `color-session/space-catalog.ts` exporting a single `readonly SpaceEntry[]` typed `satisfies Record<DisplayColorSpace, SpaceEntry>` where SpaceEntry carries id · label · channels · info · doc · interpolatable. Adding a SpaceId then fails to compile until its doc and info exist. All three fallbacks and both `as any`/`as keyof` casts delete; INTERPOLATION_SPACES becomes CATALOG.filter(e => e.interpolatable).

### `DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D) — demo/color-picker/App.vue, the app sh` · D-1 · DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D)

**Defect.** On mobile, nine named and enabled action-bar controls are completely inert, plus every colorPickerRef-dependent affordance. App.vue attaches PaneSlot's `:on-mount` only to the two DESKTOP slots, so the four pane-instance refs the action handlers dereference are permanently null at mobile — `null?.regenerate?.()` yields undefined with no call, no throw and no feedback.

**Mechanism.** breakpoint-forked slot plumbing — instance registration is a per-call-site prop (`:on-mount`), so liveness desynchronises with every v-if branch; `any`-typed refs (D-13) suppress the compiler diagnostic that would have caught it

**Evidence.**

```
App.vue:83-91 (mobile PaneSlot, NO :on-mount) vs App.vue:105 and App.vue:131 (:on-mount on the desktop slots). App.vue:323-328 onDesktopLeftMount is the sole writer of colorPickerRef/generatePaneRef/gradientPaneRef; App.vue:330-332 the sole writer of mixPaneRef. usePaneRouter.ts:196 `handler: () => paneRefs.generate.value?.regenerate?.()` (and :197,198,208,209,210,220,221,222 — 9 actions). Dock.vue:41 `hasAnyActionBar = !!actionBar || !!genericBar` gates rendering on metadata, not liveness, so the layer still draws. Downstream of the same null: App.vue:38 `:action-bar="colorPickerRef?.actionBarContext ?? null"`, App.vue:41-42 commit/cancel-edit, App.vue:352 usePaletteWiring(colorPickerRef, …). Measured: p3.mjs output `{"label":"mobile-390","clicked":"dispatched","specimenChanged":false,"errs":[]}` / `{"label":"desktop-1440","clicked":"dispatched","specimenChanged":true,"errs":[]}`; desktop before oklch(0.667417 0.182968 248.748459) -> after oklch(0.632577 0.21149 37.050613), mobile unchanged at oklch(0.685775 0.168594 55.603336). Canon: VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:100 "Commit uses one glass-ui action set"; :83 states "are never color-only. Role, accessible name, state/value and associated error/status are explicit."
```

**Reproduction.** node <scratchpad>/p3.mjs  — webkit, http://localhost:9000/#/generate, waits 3000ms, dispatches a synthetic MouseEvent('click') on the nav.dock-band button whose aria-label/textContent matches /regenerate/i (the button is in a collapsed DockLayer so a real .click() times out), then diffs the first 10 `main [style*='background-color']` style attributes. Ran at viewport 390x844@3 isMobile and 1440x900. Mobile: no change. Desktop: palette regenerates.

**Proposed cure.** Do not pass instance capture per call site. `usePaneRouter` already owns the route table and the paneRefs record — let it own registration too: expose one `bindPane(slotName)` capture that every PaneSlot call site passes through, so a slot cannot mount without registering and the breakpoint cannot fork liveness. Type PaneSlot's onMount generically over the mounted component's instance type so the gap is a compile error, not a silent no-op. Under D-2's single-scene shell the branch disappears and the question stops existing.

### `DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D) — demo/color-picker/App.vue, the app sh` · D-2 · DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D)

**Defect.** The entire left/right dual-pane workspace architecture the file is built on is retired by the tranche constitution, by name and in four separate clauses. Thirteen of fourteen views ship a companion pane; all five Admin views ship the exact `right: "palettes"` companion §7 says must be REMOVED rather than restyled; About is rendered as Picker's companion which §3.1 explicitly forbids; two canonical member routes (/about, /easing) do not exist.

**Mechanism.** a local chassis recipe standing in for the producer's InstrumentChassis composition — one hand-rolled stage/inspector split with its own ratio, collapse rule and reserve, applied uniformly to eleven members that the canon requires to be separately composed

**Evidence.**

```
VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:89 "V retires the global Dock `PaneSegmentedControl` and left/right view state."; :32 "no global pane selector, left/right split state, or simultaneous two-stage miniature survives."; :218 "Each Admin route uses the full main width: the current Palettes companion, right label and resulting mobile pane selector are removed rather than restyled."; :58 "About is a quiet trailing destination rather than Picker's companion."; :38 InstrumentChassis is the housing contract and "value.js composes regions with domain content; it does not clone a local chassis recipe."; :56 "Picker cannot stand in for About." Live table demo/shell/viewSchema.ts: right:"about" (:107), right:"palettes" (:116,126,135,154,163,190,199,208,217,226), right:"mix" (:144), right:"blob" (:181), right:null only at :172 (/atmosphere). Rendered by App.vue:113-137; mobilePaneIndex written at App.vue:41-42; the `Picker | About` selector is visible in shots/safari-mobile-light/picker.png. Member-route inventory fixed at exactly eleven incl. /about and /easing (VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:58) — neither is in viewSchema.ts.
```

**Reproduction.** grep -n 'right:' demo/shell/viewSchema.ts  → 13 of 14 non-null; then compare against VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:218 for the five /admin/* rows (:190,199,208,217,226). Visual: shots/safari-desktop-light/picker.png (About as Picker's equal companion) and shots/safari-mobile-light/picker.png (the retired pane selector).

**Proposed cure.** Transpose, do not patch. App.vue becomes a single-scene shell: `<nav>` dock band + one `<main>` + one landmark-neutral route scene + the stable H1 (D-4). Region composition — stage/inspector/action, ratio, collapse behaviour, mobile order — moves onto glass-ui's InstrumentChassis per route exactly as §3.1's table already binds. The `left`/`right` axis, `mobilePaneIndex`, `pane-wrapper--left/--right`, the ghost slot, `pane-container--dual` and the desktop/mobile v-if fork all die in one cut, collapsing D-1, D-3, D-7, D-10 and D-12 with them.

### `DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D) — demo/color-picker/App.vue, the app sh` · D-3 · DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D)

**Defect.** The desktop two-part scene renders exactly 50.0000% / 50.0000%. The canon permits only `golden` (61.8033989/38.1966011) or `preview-dominant` (66.6666667/33.3333333), and binds Picker specifically to the exact golden inspector. Equal columns are shipped by written intent, so nothing in the frame identifies the protagonist.

**Mechanism.** the shell owns grid-template-columns, so one ratio is imposed on eleven differently-composed members; ratio is a property of the route composition, not of the shell

**Evidence.**

```
Measured at 1440x900 (probe-app-D.mjs, desktop-1440): `paneGridCols = "512px 512px"`, left {x:199,w:512}, right {x:729,w:512}, left share = 50.0000%. Delta from the nearest legal ratio = 11.80 percentage points. Source: demo/styles/shell.css:133-138 `.pane-container--dual { /* Equal columns always (the kept 1fr<->1fr invariant) */ grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(var(--pane-min), 1fr)); }`. Canon: VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:27 (§3 law 1) and :43 (§3.1 Picker row, "exact golden inspector 38.1966011%"); also :28 (§3 law 2) "Empty secondary content ... never receives half the viewport" — every right:"palettes" route with an empty library receives exactly half. PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:48 PR-04 requires Admin companion 50%->0.
```

**Reproduction.** node <scratchpad>/probe-app-D.mjs  → row label "desktop-1440": getComputedStyle('.pane-container').gridTemplateColumns === '512px 512px'; getBoundingClientRect widths 512/512 inside a 1042px container.

**Proposed cure.** Move the ratio onto InstrumentChassis as named typed values (`golden` | `preview-dominant`) per VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:40-52's table, and have the shell stop declaring grid-template-columns at all. The 50/50 grid then cannot outlive the composition that chose it.

### `DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D) — demo/color-picker/App.vue, the app sh` · D-4 · DESIGN (CHALLENGE-D)

**Defect.** There is no `<h1>` anywhere in demo/. The shell owns the `<main>` landmark but not the route heading, so every row of the canon's navigation-focus contract targets a node that does not exist. There is also no skip link.

**Mechanism.** landmark ownership split — the shell claims `<main>` but delegates identity to the panes, so no node satisfies the H1 half of a contract that names the shell as its owner

**Evidence.**

```
`grep -rln "<h1" demo/` → no output (zero files). Measured h1Count = 0 at 1440x900, 720x450@2x and 390x844 (probe-app-D.mjs, all three rows). Tracked matrix agrees: the `h1` column in REPORT.md:119-178 is 0 for all 60 captures. `grep -rn "skip-link\|Skip to" demo/` → empty. mainCount = 1 everywhere (REPORT.md:27 mainCountNotOne=0) so the landmark half IS correct — App.vue:47. Canon: VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:85 "Each route has one H1 and exactly one stable main landmark, owned by the shell"; :118 "The shell `<main>` and route H1 are stable nodes"; the §5.1 table at :108-116 resolves focus to "destination H1" in four of seven rows. STATES.json keyboard-focus-desktop rows report focused: "body[…]" after 12 Tabs — the active element is still document.body (which is also the mount host, D-9).
```

**Reproduction.** grep -rln '<h1' demo/  (empty) ; node <scratchpad>/probe-app-D.mjs  → h1Count 0 on all three matrices ; awk on REPORT.md:119-178 h1 column → 0/60.

**Proposed cure.** The shell renders one `<h1>` inside `<main>`, fed by the same route identity `installDocumentTitle` already composes (router/useDocumentTitle.ts:62) — one source, two sinks (`<title>` + H1) — plus the temporary `tabindex="-1"` focus seat §5.1 requires and a skip link as the shell's first tabbable node. Both belong to App.vue and nowhere else.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defect hunt on `demo/palettes/b` · C-1 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The swatch is not an interactive element: `tag="button"`, `:aria-label` and `@click.stop` are all discarded by glass-ui `WatercolorDot`, which declares `inheritAttrs: false` and renders a hard-coded `<span aria-hidden="true">` with inline `pointer-events: none`. Swatch click is dead on fine pointers; the reka-ui `PopoverTrigger as-child` touch path is dead identically (as-child props arrive as fallthrough attrs); every swatch is nameless and aria-hidden.

**Mechanism.** glass-ui 7 contract break absorbed silently: a child with `inheritAttrs:false` swallows every fallthrough attr/listener/ARIA the consumer sends, and the consumer invents a `tag` prop the child never declared — semantics asserted in source, absent in the DOM

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/browser/card/SwatchHoverMenu.vue:14-20,29-36 (props sent) · node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/components/watercolor-dot/WatercolorDot.vue.d.ts (declared props are exactly {color,variant,animate,cycleDuration,range,seed} — no `tag`) · node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/watercolor-dot.js:80 `inheritAttrs: !1,` and :102-110 renders `o("span", {"aria-hidden":"true", … pointerEvents:"none"})` · live measurement: {"tagName":"SPAN","ariaHidden":"true","ariaLabel":null,"tagAttr":null,"attrs":["data-v-292b9032","aria-hidden","class","data-testid","data-variant","style"],"pointerEvents":"none","tabIndex":-1,"parentTag":"DIV","parentAttrs":["class=relative"]} · click probe: {"hitTest":"DIV.relative","panelsBefore":0,"panelsAfterClick":0} · Playwright actionability: `<div class="relative"> intercepts pointer events` (5000ms timeout)
```

**Reproduction.** 1) seed localStorage['color-palettes'] with a local palette before boot; 2) load http://localhost:9000/#/palettes; 3) click a card to expand; 4) `document.querySelector('.watercolor-swatch')` → SPAN, aria-hidden=true, aria-label null, pointer-events none; 5) dispatch a click at the swatch centre → `document.querySelectorAll('.floating-panel').length` stays 0

**Proposed cure.** Move the semantics to the consumer, not the primitive: SwatchHoverMenu renders its own `<button type="button" :aria-label>` and mounts `<WatercolorDot>` inside it as the pure decoration it already is (its `aria-hidden` + `pointer-events:none` then become correct by construction). One element, one file; removes all dependence on a `tag` prop glass-ui never offered. Adding `tag` to glass-ui would be the edict-4-compliant alternative but violates KISS — a wrapping `<button>` buys the same thing for free.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defect hunt on `demo/palettes/b` · C-2 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** `.floating-panel` — the class the swatch hover popover is built on — does not exist in the demo or in glass-ui 7.0.0. It was deleted in commit c84504d3 (2026-03-25) with the message "Delete dock.css, floating-panel.css, glass.css, transitions.css; now provided by @import @mkbabb/glass-ui/styles". It is not provided. The panel renders `position: static` (making `positionPanel`'s inline top/left inert), fully transparent, with no shadow/radius/z-index, appended at the end of `<body>`, half off the left edge.

**Mechanism.** a migration commit deleted a demo-local stylesheet on an unverified claim that the upstream package supplies it; the JS positioning code (viewport coords, written for `position: fixed`) was left behind and is now writing inert values onto a static element

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/browser/card/SwatchHoverMenu.vue:42 `class="floating-panel"` · `grep -rln "floating-panel" node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/` → no matches · live computed style: {"position":"static","top":"0px","left":"0px","zIndex":"auto","background":"rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)","boxShadow":"none","borderRadius":"0px"}, panelRect {"x":-195,"y":844,"w":390,"h":40} in viewport {"w":390,"h":844} at scrollY 0, parent BODY · `git show c84504d3^:demo/@/styles/floating-panel.css` recovers: `/* Floating panel — used by PaletteCard swatch popover & PaletteCardMenu (Teleported) */ .floating-panel { position: fixed; z-index: var(--z-overlay); border-radius: var(--radius-xl); border: 1px solid hsl(var(--border)/0.6); background: hsl(var(--card)/0.75); backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3); box-shadow: var(--glass-shadow-elevated); pointer-events: auto; animation: floating-panel-in …; }`
```

**Reproduction.** 1) seed a local palette; 2) /#/palettes; 3) expand a card; 4) real-mouse hover a swatch wrapper; 5) `getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.floating-panel')).position` → "static", backgroundColor → "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)", getBoundingClientRect() → {x:-195, y:844}

**Proposed cure.** Do NOT re-add a demo-local floating-panel.css — that reinstates the very fork the migration meant to absorb, and a popover is a component-type glass-ui already owns (edict 4). Retire the hand-rolled Teleport branch entirely and render the reka-ui `Popover` on BOTH pointer classes, driving `:open` from `useHoverPopover` on fine pointers. That single removal deletes `positionPanel`, deletes the `floatingStyle` prop and its type drift, deletes the aria-hidden panel (C-9), and cures C-6.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defect hunt on `demo/palettes/b` · C-3 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** A malformed palette colour destroys the whole pane: `safeFirstColor` calls `certifyAccentInk`, which handles `parseCssColor`'s `!ok` result but not its THROW. `parseCssColor` throws a TypeError on the entire empty-argument colour-function class (`oklch()`, `rgb()`, `hsl()`, `lab()`, `lch()`, `oklab()`, `hwb()`, `color()`, `rgba()`, whitespace variants, case variants), and the API persists any string up to 200 chars with no colour validation.

**Mechanism.** a Result-returning parser that is actually partial — it throws instead of returning `{ok:false}` for a whole input class — so every caller's `!ok` degenerate is bypassed; this is the standing R1 crash class reaching a render-path computed over unvalidated remote data

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/browser/card/PaletteCard/PaletteCard.vue:226-230 (`firstColor` → `safeCss` → `certifyAccentInk`) bound at :144 · demo/color-session/ink.ts:38-43,130-143 (handles `!parsed.ok`, not the throw) · api/src/modules/palette/schema.ts:28 `css: z.string().min(1).max(200)` — no colour validation · vite-node probe over 18 inputs: `threw: 13 / 18`, e.g. `"oklch()" => THREW: TypeError Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'replace')` · exact-call probe (node AND live browser via `import('/@fs/…/demo/color-session/ink.ts')`): `certifyAccentInk("oklch()", 0.45) => THREW: TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'replace')` while `"#888"` and `"not-a-color"` return normally · live: seeding colors[0].css="oklch()" and loading /#/palettes replaced the pane with "This panel hit an unexpected error. Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'replace')"
```

**Reproduction.** `npx vite-node` on `import { certifyAccentInk } from 'demo/color-session/ink.ts'; certifyAccentInk('oklch()', 0.45)` → TypeError. Live: seed `localStorage['color-palettes']` with `{version:1,palettes:[{id:'x',name:'M',slug:'m',isLocal:true,colors:[{css:'oklch()',position:0}],createdAt:…,updatedAt:…}]}`, reload /#/palettes.

**Proposed cure.** Fix the parser at the root, not the call site: `parseCssColor` must return `{ok:false}` for the empty-args class (this is R1 from the π parser-proof gate and it is still live). `certifyAccentInk` already carries the correct degenerate — nothing in PaletteCard should change. Independently, `api/src/modules/palette/schema.ts` should refine `css` through the same parser so unparseable colours never persist.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/palettes/browse` · C-1 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** Concurrent-expand race: `toggleUserExpand` writes `userPalettes` from an awaited fetch with no request-generation guard, so a late response for user A renders inside user B's open disclosure — and the admin delete then destroys A's palette while decrementing B's count.

**Mechanism.** Value not bound to its key — `expandedUserSlug` and `userPalettes` are two independent cells joined only by wall-clock order; nothing records which request a resolved value belongs to.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/browser/admin/AdminUsersPanel.vue:352-365 (`userPalettes.value = await pm.loadUserPalettes(slug)`; `finally` clears loading first-to-settle) and :150 (`@admin-delete="emit('adminDeleteUserPalette', $event, user.slug)"`). probe-impl-c3.json, BOTH engines identical: `t_betaSettled: {openRow: "beta-drifter-00022Pale", disclosure: "BETA-PALETTE-BBB 2 0"}` then `t_alphaLanded: {openRow: "beta-drifter-00022Pale", disclosure: "ALPHA-PALETTE-AAA 2 0"}`, `RACE_REPRODUCED: true`. probe-impl-c3b.json: `deleteRequests: ["http://localhost:9077/admin/palettes/alpha-p-1"]`, `VERDICT: {betaBadgeBefore:"2", betaBadgeAfter:"1", alphaBadgeBefore:"4", alphaBadgeAfter:"4"}`. useAdminUsers.ts:118-121 decrements the owner passed by the row. Frames: frames-impl/C3b-{chromium,webkit}-beta-shows-alpha.png.
```

**Reproduction.** VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:9077 npx vite --port 9077 ; node docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/AdminUsersPanel/probe-impl-c3.mjs (race, 2 engines) ; node .../probe-impl-c3b.mjs (the destroying DELETE + badge deltas). Serve alpha's /admin/users/*/palettes with 1500ms delay and beta's with 40ms; click alpha, wait 300ms, click beta.

**Proposed cure.** Do not add a sequence counter to the current shape — the shape is the defect. Express the disclosure as the keyed resource it is: a colocated `useAdminUserPalettes(slugRef)` driven by `watch(slugRef, …)` with an AbortController cancelled on key change, returning `{ palettes, loading, error }` bound to the key they were fetched for. A response for a stale key then has nowhere to land — the race becomes unrepresentable rather than policed. Side effects: supplies C-4's missing `error` cell, and removes userPalettes/removeUserPalette/updatePaletteTier/clearUserPalettes from the :389 defineExpose (the adjudicated L-1/L-2 inversion) as a consequence rather than as separate work.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/palettes/browse` · C-2 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The prune confirmation quotes a SEARCH-FILTERED count while `POST /admin/users/prune-empty` destroys every empty user in the corpus; the Prune button's enablement is wrong in both directions from the same source.

**Mechanism.** A destructive action's stated scope and its gate are derived from a client-side view filter while its effect is global — the describing authority and the executing authority are different.

**Evidence.**

```
AdminUsersPanel.vue:241 `emptyCount = computed(() => users.filter((u) => !(u.paletteCount ?? 0)).length)` over the FILTERED prop (AdminPane.vue:28 `:users="pm.filteredAdminUsers.value"`, filter at useAdminUsers.ts:30-34); :288-299 composes the copy from `emptyCount.value`; demo/palettes/api/admin-users.ts:69 `pruneEmptyUsers(token)` takes no filter/body/slug-list. probe-impl.json, BOTH engines: fixture has 3 empty users, search "gamma" → `C2_pruneDialog: "Prune 1 empty users? This will permanently delete 1 user with 0 palettes and their sessions. This cannot be undone."`. Inverse, search "alpha": `C2_filteredNoEmpty.toolbar = [{Prune empty, disabled: true}, …]` while 3 empty users exist. probe-impl-c8.json at scale: 137-user roster → dialog promises 10.
```

**Reproduction.** node docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/AdminUsersPanel/probe-impl.mjs (cells C2_unfiltered / C2_filtered / C2_pruneDialog / C2_filteredNoEmpty). Frames: frames-impl/C2-{chromium,webkit}-prune-dialog-filtered.png.

**Proposed cure.** A destructive action's stated scope must come from the authority that executes it: either the server answers a preview (`GET /admin/users/prune-empty` → `{count}`) that the dialog quotes and the button gates on, or the client sends the explicit slug list it just described. Renaming the computed or passing the unfiltered array does not fix the split; the description and the effect must be the same query. (Riders visible in the same pasted string: the title never pluralises — "Prune 1 empty users?" — while the description does.)

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-1 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The confirmation dialog for the irreversible "Prune empty" bulk deletion states a count derived from the search-FILTERED client list, while the action it guards is an unscoped server-wide mutation. It promises fewer deletions than it performs.

**Mechanism.** Truth derived at the wrong altitude: the leaf computes scope from whatever array it was handed instead of receiving the server's scope.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/browser/admin/AdminUsersPanel.vue:241 (emptyCount over the `users` prop) + demo/palettes/admin/AdminPane.vue:28 (users = pm.filteredAdminUsers) + demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:29-48 (filtered+sorted) vs demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:190 (pruneEmptyUsers(token) — no scope arg). Probe output: D-filtered {"headerBadge":"6","toolbar":["6 users","· 1 empty"],"renderedRows":1}; D-dialog "Prune 1 empty users?\n\nThis will permanently delete 1 user with 0 palettes and their sessions. This cannot be undone."; D-after POST /admin/users/prune-empty → toolbarAfter ["Pruned 3 users"]. Frame docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/AdminUsersPanel/frames-D2/D-confirm-prune-filtered-dark.png. Aggravated by demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:59 listUsers(token, 50) — even unfiltered the number is a 50-row page count. Law: VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:218 ("dangerous confirmation … Actor, scope, provenance and effect remain visible"); PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:55 PR-11.
```

**Reproduction.** node docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/AdminUsersPanel/probe-D2.mjs (section D). Manually: #/admin/users with an admin token and ≥2 empty users, type a query matching exactly ONE empty user, click "Prune empty", read the dialog, confirm, read the result line.

**Proposed cure.** Confirm against the server's scope, not a client-derived count: the prune affordance resolves the candidate set (GET the empty users), the dialog NAMES the candidates, and the confirmed call carries those identities — the two-phase review anatomy PR-11 already prescribes. Reading the unfiltered array instead only swaps a wrong number for a differently wrong one, because that array is a 50-row page.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-2 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** A failed destructive mutation renders as a benign, self-contradicting success message. A 500 on the prune endpoint produces "No empty users to prune" while the same toolbar simultaneously reads "· 2 empty". No error surface at all.

**Mechanism.** The result channel's type cannot express failure: onPruneDone(count: number) collapses {refused} and {nothing to do} into the same integer, so no downstream design can distinguish them.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:195-198 (catch → return 0) meeting demo/palettes/browser/admin/AdminUsersPanel.vue:303-309 (count > 0 ? "Pruned N" : "No empty users to prune"). Probe output with the endpoint fulfilled 500 application/problem+json: {"message":"No empty users to prune","toolbar":["3 users","· 2 empty"],"rowsStillRendered":3,"anyErrorRole":false}. Frame frames-D2/G-prune-FAILED-desktop-light.png. The component already ratified the opposite principle for the LOAD path at AdminUsersPanel.vue:49-50 ("error ≠ empty — a dead backend never costumes as an empty roster") and :51-62 (EmptyState variant="error").
```

**Reproduction.** node docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/AdminUsersPanel/probe-D2d-prune-failure.mjs — intercepts POST **/prune-empty with a 500 and reads the rendered result span.

**Proposed cure.** Widen the result to the three outcomes the action actually has (pruned N / nothing to prune / failed + machine reason) and render failure in the error register already ratified at :51-62. The signature, not the string, is the defect.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-3 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The unauthenticated state was never designed. With no admin token the panel renders the TRUE-EMPTY plate — "· roster clear · / No users found." — a positive factual claim about a corpus the app has never queried; and the Refresh button is enabled, named, styled and completely inert.

**Mechanism.** A third state species (signed-out) was folded into an existing one (empty) instead of being designed or subtracted.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:55-56 (getAdminToken() null → return before any request) falling through to AdminUsersPanel.vue:63. Probe E: {"token":null,"adminReqsBefore":10,"adminReqsAfterRefreshClick":10,"emptyStateSays":"· ROSTER CLEAR · |  | No users found.","refreshDisabled":false,"pruneDisabled":true} — zero new requests after the click. This is the state in ALL 60 mega-tranche captures: docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/visual/REPORT.md:128,143,158,173 (text 273/273/122/122, dock reads "Login"). Law: PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:70 §5.5, :71 §5.6, :52 PR-08.
```

**Reproduction.** Open http://localhost:9000/#/admin/users with no `palette-admin-token` in localStorage; click Refresh; observe zero network activity and an unchanged "No users found." Also visible in every shipped audit screenshot at docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/visual/shots/*/admin-users.png.

**Proposed cure.** Signed-out is a route-guard species, not a roster species. The admin route should not mount a roster it cannot populate, and the panel should not own a third empty-shaped state. Subtract the state rather than annotate it.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-4 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** RTL fabricates identifiers. The hand-rolled two-span tail-priority slug split has no bidi isolation, so `empty-ghost-account-aaaa-33` renders on screen as `aaa-33empty-ghost-account-a` — a string that does not exist. The row and its own delete confirmation then display two different identities for the same account.

**Mechanism.** An identifier composed by string concatenation across two DOM nodes instead of rendered as one typed, direction-isolated value.

**Evidence.**

```
AdminUsersPanel.vue:97-101 (two sibling spans) + :246-252 (slugHead/slugTail). Probe C under dir=rtl: {"headText":"an-extremely-long-…-2","headL":1010.5,"headR":1194,"tailText":"f9a-33","tailL":950,"tailR":1010.5,"tailRenderedLeftOfHead":true}. Frame frames-D2/C-populated-rtl-desktop.png shows `aaa-33empty-ghost-account-a`, `aaa-77empty-ghost-account-a`, `bbb-11empty-three-b`. The dialog quotes the raw unsplit slug at :169. The count line likewise renders "empty 3 · users 6" (from :8-13). Law verbatim, VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:133 — slug/ID must keep "identical domain meaning inside an LTR-isolated value … no custom bidi reinterpretation"; :154 — "slugs, IDs and provenance | render in LTR-isolated spans inside RTL prose".
```

**Reproduction.** node docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/AdminUsersPanel/probe-D2.mjs (section C), or set document.documentElement.dir='rtl' on a populated #/admin/users. Also visible without data in the shipped capture docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/visual/shots/rtl-desktop/adminusers.png ("users 0").

**Proposed cure.** Delete the split entirely. Head-elision with a protected tail is one CSS declaration on one LTR-isolated text node (`direction: rtl; text-overflow: ellipsis` inside a `<bdi>`), not two spans and two string functions. That single transposition also removes the viewport-dependent ellipsis gap artefact (390: `empty-ghost-acco…  aaa-33` with a visible inter-token gap; 1440: no gap).

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-01 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** One Retry click permanently blanks the pane body. Transition mode="out-in" on the three-state container makes DOM presence a function of a 200ms animation clock, while the state machine settles synchronously; the pending enter never mounts and the pane is left as a void under the search field with no error, no empty plate, no skeleton and no recovery.

**Mechanism.** Animation clock owns DOM presence — branch identity is animated with mode="out-in" across an async boundary that can settle faster than the leave duration.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/BrowsePane.vue:40 (`<Transition name="vj-morph" mode="out-in">`); demo/platform/transport/availability.ts:188-195 (assertApiAttemptAllowed throws synchronously, unconditional for `misconfigured`). Live Playwright/WebKit 1440x900, sampled every 25ms from the click: t=0 n=1 h=224.5 "The commons is unreachable.Failed to load palettes"; t=28 n=1 vj-morph-leave-from|vj-morph-leave-active; t=227 n=1 vj-morph-leave-active|vj-morph-leave-to; t=251 n=0 h=12.0 ""; t=1202 n=0 h=12.0 "". Second clean-page run, 6s tail: {wrapChildren:0, wrapH:12, paneText:"BrowseDiscover palettes from the community."}; after typing into the search field: {wrapChildren:0, same paneText}. Screenshots: docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/components/BrowsePane/evidence/D-browse-before-retry.png vs D-browse-after-retry-6s.png.
```

**Reproduction.** 1) `http://localhost:9000/#/browse` at 1440x900 (dev server without VITE_API_URL, so the misconfigured latch is armed — the state all 60 audit captures show). 2) Wait ~3.5s for the error plate. 3) Click Retry once. 4) The state container's element children go 1→0 at ~251ms and stay 0 indefinitely; wrapper height 224.5px→12px. Typing in the search box does not restore it.

**Proposed cure.** Stop animating branch identity. Keep one persistent state region whose CONTENT cross-fades (default simultaneous mode), or drop the Transition on the state container entirely and let the individual plates own their entrance. A v-if guard inside mode="out-in" only narrows the window; it does not close the class.

**Defect.** The Browse composition is the equal-card matrix the constitution rules cannot satisfy any member, and the field wears the Card shell the same row forbids.

Visible in shots/safari-desktop-light/browse.png and shots/safari-desktop-dark/browse.png.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-03 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** No selected-palette inspector exists anywhere in the pane, so every action the constitution assigns to the inspector is crammed onto the card as a seventeen-handler omnibus with an expand-in-place selection model.

**Mechanism.** The composition row was never built — the missing inspector region forces every inspector-owned job onto the card body.

**Evidence.**

```
VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md §5: "A palette card is a bounded entity article, not a clickable role=article, listbox/option composite, or seven-mode omnibus. … The card body owns no expand, inline rename, action menu, transient result or hover-only swatch-action path. Full detail, rename/lifecycle/export actions and durable operation state live in the selected inspector." BrowsePane.vue:92-117 binds :expanded, @click=toggleExpand, @save, @delete, @vote, @rename, @edit-color, @add-color, @feature, @admin-delete, @set-visibility, @fork, @versions, @flag, @export, @edit-tags — 17 bindings. No inspector region appears in the 360-line file. §3.1 mobile order for Browse is "search, results, selected inspector"; the third term is absent. Live a11y snapshot renders the entity as `article "Palette: Featured One" [cursor=pointer]` — the clickable role=article the sentence forbids.
```

**Reproduction.** Read BrowsePane.vue:92-117 and grep the file for any inspector/detail region: none exists. Confirm on /#/browse that selection is expressed as card expansion (pm.expandedId) with no companion detail surface.

**Proposed cure.** Build the inspector the composition row specifies, move the fourteen action handlers into it, and reduce the card to identity + specimen + one native `<button type="button" aria-pressed>` seat.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** Inverted dependency: the domain/server-state composable `useAdminUsers` imports the SFC, holds a template-ref to its instance, and mutates the component's private state through 5 `defineExpose`d members. Latent module cycle; 5 optional-chain masking fallbacks.

**Mechanism.** Split ownership: `expandedUserSlug`/`userPalettes`/`loadingUserPalettes` (AdminUsersPanel.vue:236-238) are stored in the view but every mutation originates in the state layer, so the state layer must reach across the layer boundary through an instance handle.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:14 `import type { AdminUsersPanel } from "./browser/admin";` · :27 `const adminUsersPanelRef = ref<InstanceType<typeof AdminUsersPanel> | null>(null);` · :95 `adminUsersPanelRef.value?.updatePaletteTier(...)` · :117 `?.removeUserPalette(...)` · :132/:146 `?.clearUserPalettes(...)` · demo/palettes/usePalettePorts.ts:123 `admin.adminUsersPanelRef.value?.onPruneDone(pruned);` · demo/palettes/admin/AdminPane.vue:27,:134 · AdminUsersPanel.vue:389 `defineExpose({ removeUserPalette, updatePaletteTier, clearUserPalettes, onPruneDone, userPalettes })`. Measured: `grep -rn --include='*.ts' 'from "[^"]*\.vue"' demo/ | wc -l` = 25, of which 24 are barrel re-exports — useAdminUsers.ts:14 is the sole state module importing a component. `grep -rn --include='*.vue' defineExpose demo/` = 14 sites; this is the largest (5) and the only one whose consumer is a composable. tsconfig.base.json:8 `verbatimModuleSyntax: true` is why the cycle erases.
```

**Reproduction.** AdminPane.vue:26 mounts the panel under `v-if="subView === 'admin-users'"`. Call `useAdminUsers.onDeleteUser(slug)` (:140-150) and switch the admin sub-view during the `await deleteUser(token, slug)`; `adminUsersPanelRef.value` is then null and the `?.` at :146 silently swallows `clearUserPalettes(slug)`. The panel remounts with the deleted user's `userPalettes` never cleared. Same swallow at all 5 call sites.

**Proposed cure.** Lift the three refs into a `useAdminUserPalettes()` composable colocated with `useAdminUsers`; the panel receives them as props and emits `toggleUserExpand`. Then delete AdminUsersPanel.vue:352-389 (4 state fns + defineExpose), useAdminUsers.ts:14/:27 and the 4 `?.` call sites, usePalettePorts.ts:123/:197, AdminPane.vue:27/:134. Net: −5 exposed members, −1 port member, −1 inverted import, −1 latent cycle, −5 masking fallbacks.

---

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** PaletteCard feeds arbitrary user/API color strings into `certifyAccentInk`, a demo-side wrapper that converts the library's typed Result error into a thrown exception during render. Any palette color carrying alpha < 1 destroys the whole hosting pane via the error boundary; zero cards render and the poison is persisted in localStorage.

**Mechanism.** Ownership inversion at the library boundary: the library owns 'certification may fail, here is a typed error'; demo/color-session/ink.ts unilaterally re-declares it as 'cannot fail' and PaletteCard consumes that re-declaration on untrusted data. A total function was made partial at the wrong layer.

**Evidence.**

```
PaletteCard.vue:226 `firstColor = props.palette.colors[0]?.css ?? …`; PaletteCard.vue:229-230 `const { safeCss } = useSafeAccentFn("well"); const safeFirstColor = computed(() => safeCss(firstColor.value))`; demo/color-session/ink.ts:78 `if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`Ink certification failed: ${result.error.code}`)`; src/color/operations.ts:213-214 `if (accent.alpha !== 1 || surface.alpha !== 1) return err({ code: "color_invalid_input" })`. node against the served dist: `lab(50% 20 10 / 82.7%) => safeAccent ERR color_invalid_input`, `oklch(0.7 0.1 200 / 0.5) => ERR color_invalid_input`, `#e11d48 => OK`. Live: pane shows `This panel hit an unexpected error. / color_non_finite`, articles: 0. The app's own persisted state held `color-picker = {"inputColor":"lab(92% 88.8 20 / 82.7%)"}`, i.e. it mints alpha'd colors itself.
```

**Reproduction.** With the dev server live at http://localhost:9000: (1) in the page, `localStorage.setItem('color-palettes', JSON.stringify({version:1,palettes:[{id:'p',slug:'p',name:'Probe',isLocal:true,colors:[{css:'lab(50% 20 10 / 82.7%)',position:0},{css:'#0ea5e9',position:1}]}]}))`; (2) full reload of `/#/palettes`, settle 2s. Observed `{url:'http://localhost:9000/#/palettes', boundary:true, bodyHead:'…This panel hit an unexpected error.\n\ncolor_non_finite…', articles:0}`. Repeats with `lab(92% 88.8 20 / 82.7%)`. Control with `['#e11d48','#0ea5e9']` → no boundary. (Exact throw site among ink.ts:47/59/78 is a hypothesis; the Result-to-throw class is confirmed.)

**Proposed cure.** Make `certifyAccentInk` TOTAL on dynamic input — return the input css (or the muted-ink token) for every non-certifiable value, exactly as it already does for unparseable input at ink.ts:136, and add the alpha pre-condition the library states at operations.ts:214. Keep throws only for producer literals (ink.ts:53), which are build-time invariants. Then no ink consumer can crash a pane and PaletteCard needs no guard at all.

---

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The state layer imports the view and drives it through a template ref. `useAdminUsers` (a server-state composable) imports the SFC's type, holds its INSTANCE, and mutates the component's private state via defineExpose'd methods; the instance handle is published on the app-wide ADMIN_PORT_KEY surface. NEW consequence this pass: because the mutators live where the lists do not, `onAdminDeleteUserPalette` updates the panel's copy but never `remotePalettes` — Browse keeps rendering a deleted palette.

**Mechanism.** Split semantic ownership across a layer boundary: storage of "the expanded admin user's palettes" lives in the view (AdminUsersPanel.vue:236-238), mutation lives in the composable — so the lower layer must reach up through an instance handle. Yields a type-level cycle (erased only by `import type` + verbatimModuleSyntax), five masking `?.` swallows, and mutators that see one list but not the other.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/useAdminUsers.ts:14 `import type { AdminUsersPanel } from "./browser/admin";` · :27 `ref<InstanceType<typeof AdminUsersPanel>|null>` · :95/:117/:132/:146 `adminUsersPanelRef.value?.<method>()` · demo/palettes/usePalettePorts.ts:123 `admin.adminUsersPanelRef.value?.onPruneDone(pruned)` · :197 publishes the handle on adminPort · demo/palettes/admin/AdminPane.vue:27 `ref="adminUsersPanelRef"` + :134 `const adminUsersPanelRef = pm.adminUsersPanelRef` (the pane BORROWS the composable's ref) · AdminUsersPanel.vue:389 defineExpose of 5 members incl. mutable `userPalettes`. Divergence proof by contradiction: useAdminUsers.ts:106 (Browse path) filters `deps.remotePalettes`, while :112-125 (admin path) does not — yet the sibling :87 searches `remotePalettes` for a palette sourced from THIS panel's card (AdminUsersPanel.vue:149 -> AdminPane.vue:37), so the codebase itself asserts the palette is in both lists. Uniqueness measured: of 25 .ts->.vue edges in demo/, 24 are barrels; this is the only state module importing a component.
```

**Reproduction.** Divergence (exact steps; NOT executed — needs an admin token and the step is destructive, this seat was read-only): admin-authenticate -> #/browse, note palette P -> #/admin/users, expand P's owner -> delete P from the row -> #/browse: P is still listed. Swallow variant (r1, mechanically certain): trigger onDeleteUser then switch admin sub-view during the await (AdminPane.vue:26 v-if unmounts the panel) -> `adminUsersPanelRef.value` is null -> clearUserPalettes silently skipped.

**Proposed cure.** Transposition: lift expandedUserSlug/userPalettes/loadingUserPalettes into a focused `useAdminUserPalettes()` beside useAdminUsers; panel receives them as props and emits toggleUserExpand. Delete AdminUsersPanel.vue:389, :352-387, useAdminUsers.ts:14/:27 and the four `?.` sites, usePalettePorts.ts:123/:197, AdminPane.vue:27/:134. Net: -5 exposed members, -1 port member, -1 inverted edge, -1 latent cycle, -5 masking fallbacks, and the remotePalettes divergence becomes unrepresentable because one module sees both lists.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-15 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** `.floating-panel` — the class that is supposed to make the swatch hover popover a floating surface — is defined by NO module: not in demo/styles/, not anywhere in the repo's CSS, not in @mkbabb/glass-ui 7.0.0. The teleported popover therefore computes to position:static with no background, shadow or z-index, so its inline top/left are inert and it lands in normal flow at the end of <body>.

**Mechanism.** Ownership vacuum / dangling design-system reference. Glass 7.0.0 was 'adopted whole' at W44; `.floating-panel` was a glass-ui recipe the demo never owned and glass-ui 7 does not ship. No toolchain can see it — a CSS class is a string.

**Evidence.**

```
SwatchHoverMenu.vue:37-51 applies `class="floating-panel"` + inline :style. `grep -rn "floating-panel" --include='*.css' . --exclude-dir=node_modules` → one hit, and it is a COMMENT (demo/styles/animations.css:2). `grep -rln "floating-panel" node_modules/@mkbabb/` → no output. Live probe (synthetic element + full document.styleSheets scan): {"computed":{"position":"static","transform":"none","background":"rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)","zIndex":"auto","border":"0px","boxShadow":"none"},"rules":[],"ruleCount":0}. Measured rendered geometry: rect {x:-720, y:900, w:1440, h:40}, parentIsBody:true. Spec: CSS Positioned Layout L3, `static` — "the box's top, right, bottom, and left properties have no effect."
```

**Reproduction.** http://localhost:9000/#/palettes with ≥1 saved palette in localStorage key `color-palettes` → click a card to expand → hover any swatch. Popover renders as a transparent full-viewport-width strip at the bottom of the document instead of a panel over the swatch.

**Proposed cure.** Delete the hand-rolled hover branch entirely. glass-ui 7 ships ./popover and SwatchHoverMenu ALREADY uses it on the touch branch (line 61). Fold the swatch + its popover into ONE glass-ui `Swatch` component that owns its own surface; `.floating-panel` ceases to exist rather than being redefined in demo/.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-16 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** useHoverPopover reads `e.currentTarget` inside a nextTick callback — i.e. after event dispatch has completed, when the DOM spec guarantees it is null. Every swatch hover throws `TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'getBoundingClientRect')` and the popover's position is never applied. The `as Element` cast is the masking assertion that hid it from vue-tsc.

**Mechanism.** Hand-rolled floating positioning inside a demo composable, duplicating @mkbabb/glass-ui/popover which the same component already imports and uses on its other branch. A dual path (edict 2) reimplementing a design-system primitive (edict 4); the `as Element` cast (edict 2 — masking fallback) let the null flow through typecheck.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/browser/card/composables/useHoverPopover.ts:30 — `nextTick(() => positionPanel(e.currentTarget as Element));`. Live capture with an unhandledrejection listener: {"captured":["REJ:TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'getBoundingClientRect')"],"inline":"top: 0px; left: 0px; transform: translateX(-50%);"} — the inline top/left are still the reactive initial 0px, proving positionPanel never completed. Spec: DOM Standard §2.2 — "The currentTarget attribute … When an event is dispatched, this is set to the object whose listener is being invoked, and reset to null afterwards."
```

**Reproduction.** http://localhost:9000/#/palettes, seed one palette, expand the card, dispatch/perform pointerenter on a swatch wrapper with `window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', …)` installed. The rejection fires on every hover, on every desktop browser (canHover = matchMedia('(hover: hover)')).

**Proposed cure.** Capture the element at dispatch time (`const el = e.currentTarget as Element` BEFORE nextTick) is the patch; the transposition is to delete the branch — let the design-system Popover anchor to its own trigger so no coordinate is ever computed in demo/ at all.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The V.W51 byte-exact export contract (demo/palettes/export/, 12 modules) is dead in production — only a test consumes it — while BrowsePane's live export routes through the pre-contract demo/palettes/export.ts. Dual path where the tested implementation ships nothing and the shipping implementation has no byte test.

**Mechanism.** dual-path / ownership-duplication: one concept (palette export) with two live homes, kept from colliding by filename rather than by deletion; the contract-backed home has zero production consumers so its test is a false proof.

**Evidence.**

```
$ grep -rn "export/serializers" demo src test → demo/test/export/byte-exact.test.ts:23 (ONLY hit). $ grep -rn 'from "./export"' demo → demo/palettes/usePaletteExport.ts:9 (ONLY hit). BrowsePane.vue:198,324 imports/calls usePaletteExport. demo/palettes/export/serializers.ts:6-9 names its rival: "the sibling legacy `../export.ts` (the pre-contract routed seat that W50 will replace) still resolves `./export`". export/png.ts:11 imports @mkbabb/value.js/color; export.ts:85-119 uses SVG→Image→canvas and imports no value.js.
```

**Reproduction.** grep -rn "export/serializers" demo src test  # → 1 hit, a test file
grep -rn 'from "./export"' demo             # → 1 hit, usePaletteExport.ts:9

**Proposed cure.** Delete demo/palettes/export.ts and demo/palettes/usePaletteExport.ts. onExport becomes: build an ExportSnapshot from the Palette, then serializeJson|Css|Tailwind|Svg|Png + filenameFor/mimeFor from export/canonical.ts. The download side-effect moves to export/download.ts. One home, and the existing byte test then guards shipping code.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-1 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The hero readout renders `contenteditable="true"` on a span whose children Vue owns and patches (`.fig-int`/`.fig-frac`). Any user edit replaces those nodes; every later patch writes into detached nodes and silently no-ops, so the cell is permanently orphaned from the vdom and displays a value that is not the color.

**Mechanism.** Vue-controlled subtree made user-writable: contenteditable lets the browser mutate DOM that the virtual DOM believes it owns, so vnode-held element references go stale and patching becomes a no-op.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/picker/display/ColorComponentDisplay/ColorComponentDisplay.vue:21-39. Live Playwright probe against http://localhost:9000/#/ returned: {"afterTyping":["45","20.1","10"],"afterTypingInnerHTML":"<b>45</b>","afterSliderDrag_cells":["45","20.1","10"],"afterSliderDrag_innerHTML":"<b>45</b>","afterSliderDrag_meter":["43.8%","20.1","10.0","100.0%"],"url":"http://localhost:9000/#/?space=lab&color=lab(43.8%25+20.05+10)"} — URL, page title and channel meter all say 43.8; the hero readout says 45. The `<b>` wrapper proves the browser destroyed Vue's two child spans (and that the field accepts arbitrary rich text/paste).
```

**Reproduction.** 1) Open http://localhost:9000/#/ . 2) Click the `l` readout cell, press Cmd+A, type `45`, wait 1200ms. 3) Focus the L channel slider thumb (`.channel-slider [role="slider"]` first) and press ArrowLeft 12 times. 4) Read `document.querySelectorAll('.readout-fig')[0].innerText` (= "45") against `location.href` and `.channel-meter` (= 43.8%).

**Proposed cure.** Architectural transposition, already half-ordered by W48 §Work-1: delete the contenteditable path entirely; render the tuple as a read-only numeric headline and put editing in real `<input type="number">` controls. A Vue-controlled subtree may never be contenteditable — there is no patch that makes it safe. Also strengthen W48's completion gate: its `agree in one settled routed frame` check is single-frame and would pass with this defect fully present; it must edit a cell, then change the color by an independent path, then assert agreement.

---

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-2 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** All five channels share ONE 500ms debounce timer instance, so a numeric edit to channel A silently cancels a pending edit to channel B. User edits are lost with no error and no visual signal.

**Mechanism.** Debounce over a multiplexed argument: the primitive assumes successive calls express the same pending intent, but the `component` argument makes each call a distinct intent, so coalescing them is data loss rather than throttling.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/color-session/useColorPipeline.ts:209 (`const updateColorComponentDebounced = debounce(updateColorComponent, 500)`) over the single-timer closure at demo/shared/utils.ts:22-44; both ColorPicker.vue:51 and ColorPicker.vue:233 route every channel through that one instance. Unit repro via `npx tsx`: an `l` write at t=0 and an `a` write at t=100ms produced `applied writes: [[80,"a"]]` — the `l` write never executed. Live repro from `lab(80% 20 30)`: typed `30` into `l`, then 120ms later `10` into `b`; final `url":"http://localhost:9000/#/?space=lab&color=lab(80%25+20+10)"` — b took 10, l is still 80, the `30` is gone.
```

**Reproduction.** Open http://localhost:9000/#/?space=lab&color=lab(80%25+20+30) . Click the `l` readout cell, Cmd+A, type `30`. Within 500ms click the `b` cell, Cmd+A, type `10`. Wait 1.5s and read location.href — it reads lab(80% 20 10); the l edit is discarded.

**Proposed cure.** Key the debounce per channel (`Map<string, debounced>`) — or, KISS and preferred with C-1's cure, drop the debounce entirely: real `<input>` editors commit on change/blur and need no coalescing. Note W48 §Work-3 makes 'W21 numeric fields the sole editors' without noticing this, so landing those fields on the same shared instance would reship the defect in new markup.

---

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-3 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The window keydown listener is registered in `onMounted` while the component lives inside `<KeepAlive>`, so `onUnmounted` never fires on deactivation. The listener stays live on every other route and calls `e.preventDefault()`, hijacking Cmd+K app-wide.

**Mechanism.** KeepAlive lifecycle mismatch: mount/unmount hooks used where activate/deactivate hooks are required, so a global side effect outlives the component's visible lifetime.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:376-385 (onMounted/onUnmounted) under demo/shell/PaneSlot.vue:120-127 (`<KeepAlive :max="6">`), mounted at demo/color-picker/App.vue:101-110. Live probe after SPA-internal navigation to #/gradient: {"afterSwitch":{"hash":"#/gradient","pickerInDom":false,"gradientPresent":true},"events":[{"k":"Meta","meta":true,"dp":false},{"k":"k","meta":true,"dp":true}],"pickerExpanded":"absent"} — the picker is NOT in the document yet its handler still ran and still returned defaultPrevented=true. The correct idiom is used in the same directory: demo/picker/visual/HeroBlob.vue:246 uses onActivated() and explains why at :232-245.
```

**Reproduction.** 1) Load http://localhost:9000/#/ and wait for the picker to mount. 2) `location.hash = '#/gradient'` (SPA route change, no reload); wait 2.5s; confirm `document.querySelector('.title-row button')` is null. 3) Install a window keydown logger, press Meta+k. 4) The logged event has defaultPrevented === true. Cmd+K is now swallowed on /gradient, /browse, /extract, /mix, /generate, /atmosphere, /blob and all five /admin/* routes.

**Proposed cure.** Move the listener to `onActivated`/`onDeactivated`, and re-scope the shortcut off `window` onto the picker's own subtree so it cannot be a global keyboard claim in the first place.

---

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-4 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** `onStartEdit` calls the throwing `parseColor` on untrusted, API-sourced palette CSS with no guard, from inside a bare setTimeout that Vue's ErrorBoundary cannot catch. The throw leaves `preEditModel` populated and `editTarget` null — the edit machine wedges and the click is dead.

**Mechanism.** A throwing parse wrapper with no single enforced boundary: half the call sites remember to catch, half do not, and the unguarded ones sit in async callbacks outside the framework's error path.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:282-289; `parseColor` -> `parsePickerColor` throws at demo/color-session/picker-color.ts:109-113. Call site is demo/color-picker/composables/usePaletteWiring.ts:113-122, inside `setTimeout(...)` — outside Vue error propagation, so `<ErrorBoundary>` (App.vue:50) cannot catch it. Measured parser behaviour via `npx tsx` against src/css/index.ts: "oklch()" THREW TypeError Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'replace'); "light-dark(#fff, #000)" NOT-OK; "color-mix(in oklab, red, blue)" NOT-OK; "currentcolor" NOT-OK; "#12345" NOT-OK. The same hole exists on the second exposed entry point: useColorPipeline.ts:182-188 (`applyExternalColor`) parses unguarded and usePaletteWiring.ts:122-123 calls it directly — while onPaletteAddColor/onPaletteApply/applyColorString (useColorPipeline.ts:212-257) DO try/catch, showing the guarding is inconsistent.
```

**Reproduction.** Parser class is reproduced directly: `npx tsx -e 'import {parseCssColor} from "/Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js/src/css/index.ts"; parseCssColor("oklch()")'` throws TypeError. End-to-end: a stored palette color of `color-mix(in oklab, red, blue)`, `light-dark(#fff,#000)`, `currentcolor` or `oklch()` returned by api.color.babb.dev, then clicking that swatch's edit affordance, hits ColorPicker.vue:286 unguarded. (Full end-to-end not driven live — the local API is CORS-blocked in this dev config per REPORT.md:17 — so the end-to-end arm is labelled a hypothesis; the parser arm is measured.)

**Proposed cure.** Collapse the parse boundary to one place: return the `Result` the library already produces and let call sites branch, instead of a throwing wrapper. Until then, guard onStartEdit and applyExternalColor and surface a real failure to the user rather than an unhandled window error.

---

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-01 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The `contenteditable` numeric headline is an unguarded write path with no commit, cancel, clamp, or error semantics. Display, AT announcement, URL and document title disagree after a single keystroke, and the card-lock the whole reservation system exists to hold breaks mid-typing.

**Mechanism.** Editing job fused into the display job: one DOM node is simultaneously the reactive render target and the user's text-entry surface, and the model clamp/format lives at the pipeline seam, downstream of the paint. Canon: VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §7 ("read-only contiguous numeric readout"), §4 ("editing occurs only in W21's semantic numeric fields"), PROPORTION-AUDIT §5.10 ("no duplicate contenteditable path").

**Evidence.**

```
demo/picker/display/ColorComponentDisplay/ColorComponentDisplay.vue:22-23 (contenteditable="true" role="textbox"); demo/picker/ColorPicker.vue:223-235 (onComponentInput → parseFloat → updateColorComponentDebounced). Live Playwright transcript at 1440x900, route #/?space=lab&color=lab(50% 20 30): t0 cells ["50.0 % ,","20.0 ,","30.0"] sliders "a axis 20.0" title "lab(50% 20 30)"; t1 cells [...,"20.05 ,",...] ; t2(+900ms) cells [...,"20.1 ,",...] sliders "a axis 20.1" title "lab(50% 20.05 30)" url #/?space=lab&color=lab(50%25+20.05+30). Second run, insertText('9999999999'): before readout 441.8x122.4 card h 684.8 scrollW 442 = clientW 442; during readout 469.0x183.6 (+61.2px = one 1.12em line) card h 693.2 (+8.4px) scrollW 477 > clientW 469 (8px inner overflow); settled back to 122.4/684.8 with title "lab(50% 20.19999999999 30)".
```

**Reproduction.** Serve the demo (http://localhost:9000/), open #/?space=lab&color=lab(50%25%2020%2030) at 1440x900, wait for settle, focus the second [contenteditable="true"][role="textbox"] cell, collapse the selection to the end of its contents, document.execCommand('insertText', false, '5'), wait 900ms, then read the cell text, every [role=slider] aria-valuetext, location.hash and document.title. Repeat with '9999999999' while sampling .readout and the picker card getBoundingClientRect before / during / after.

**Proposed cure.** Transposition, not validation. Split the headline's three fused jobs: the route <h1> is owned by the shell, the readout becomes a pure projection (<output>, no contenteditable, no role=textbox, no tab stop), and editing moves whole into the domain-neutral axis composition over BI Slider that W48 already specifies — which owns commit/cancel/error and formats through the same cell the headline projects. One writer, one formatter, one truth. Do not add validation to the contenteditable; delete the writer.

---

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-02 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** PR-01's label→headline void is live and the register's cure is mis-apportioned: 69.93px of dead vertical air at 1440 against a 14.87px canon ceiling, of which the Blob-derived .title-row minimum contributes exactly 0px.

**Mechanism.** A display surface was made to reserve an editing worst-case: readoutReservation.ts derives lab's line count as 2 from a static worst-case ch table and `.readout` bottom-anchors a one-line value inside that two-line box, so the unused legal-width line renders as blank air above the numbers. readoutReservation.ts:138-147 models only the "cqi band (pane 400–512)" and the "390 phone" and claims the capacity is "a structural guarantee across the whole band"; at 1440 the pane IS 512 (in-band) and lab renders one line while the table derives two — the guarantee fails inside its own modelled band.

**Evidence.**

```
Live at 1440x900: trigger text-box bottom 240.91, readout first ink line top 310.84 → gap 69.93px. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §3.2 / PROPORTION-AUDIT §2.2 bind I_after ≤ min(φG, I_before − G) with G ≈ 9.2px → φG ≈ 14.87px; measured 69.93 = 4.70× the ceiling (25.4px = 1.71× at 390). Apportionment: readout reserved-minus-painted = 122.40 − 67.50 = +54.90px with align-content:flex-end (ColorComponentDisplay.vue:151,166); .picker-header row-gap = 7.168px (header.css:25); label leading ≈13.3px; .title-row min-height computes 73.12px against a content height of 84.98px → NON-binding, 0px. `--instrument-title-gap` computes to the empty string on the live page (P122 token undefined/unconsumed). Same measurement at 390: readoutReservedVsPainted = −4.2px (the lock is honest there).
```

**Reproduction.** At 1440x900 on the settled / route, take Range.getClientRects() over .space-trigger and over .readout; gap = min(readout rect tops) − max(trigger rect bottoms) = 69.93. Then compare .readout getBoundingClientRect().height (122.40) against the painted ink extent (max bottom − min top = 67.50) → 54.90px reserved-not-painted, and read getComputedStyle('.title-row').minHeight (73.12px) against its rendered height (84.98px). Repeat at 390x844 for the −4.2px arm.

**Proposed cure.** Delete `--readout-lines`, `align-content: flex-end` and the static line table outright (ColorComponentDisplay.vue:151,166 + readoutReservation.ts:122-208). One line always; a genuinely wrapped tuple grows BELOW into feature-local flow, which VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §4.2 already sanctions. The card-lock goal is then met by tabular figures + the fixed per-space least count alone — mechanism (i) that readoutReservation.ts:14-18 already names. W48 must reorder: at 1440 deleting the Blob reservation first moves the frame 0px.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-03 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The picker's primary spatial control — the 2D saturation×lightness spectrum field — is not in the tab order at all; the flagship instrument is mouse-only.

**Mechanism.** A canvas-shaped control was given a presentational role instead of a control role, so its two coordinate axes were never modelled as named numeric quantities; the composition has no shared axis primitive to fall back on.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/picker/controls/SpectrumCanvas/SpectrumCanvas.vue:8-9 — role="img" on a pointer-only div, no tabindex, no role="slider", no named numeric axes. Live enumeration of focusables inside the picker Card at 1440x900 (offsetParent !== null), in order: combobox "Select color space"; textbox "l/a/b component value" ×3; tab "l/a/b/alpha channel" ×4; slider "L/A/B/ALPHA channel" ×4. Twelve stops, none of them the spectrum. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §5 "every spatial action has a keyboard/numeric equivalent"; §5.2 "Spectrum coordinates: expose two named numeric axes … pointer canvas is not the sole keyboard control". This is V-A137, confirmed still born-RED on disk at c654824e.
```

**Reproduction.** At 1440x900 on the settled / route, enumerate [...card.querySelectorAll('a[href],button,input,select,textarea,[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"]),[contenteditable="true"]')].filter(e => e.offsetParent !== null) where card = document.querySelector('.pane-shell [data-slot=card]'); the .spectrum-picker element is absent from the result.

**Proposed cure.** Build the shared domain-neutral axis composition over BI Slider (already W48 Work step 3) and give SpectrumCanvas two named numeric S/V axes sharing one coordinate model with the canvas, per §5.2's Home/End and arrow law. The pointer canvas becomes an alternate view of the same two controls, not the only view.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-04 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The route protagonist holds exactly 50.00% of the two-pane stage — 11.8 percentage points below the canon floor — with a character-identical drop shadow to its companion, so the composition has no protagonist at all.

**Mechanism.** An equal-column grid was used where a golden instrument/inspector ratio is bound, and the companion was given the same material tier and the same shadow token as the protagonist, so neither size nor depth nor material distinguishes figure from ground.

**Evidence.**

```
Live at 1440x900: .pane-container.pane-container--dual is display:grid with grid-template-columns "512px 512px", gap 18px; panes [left x199 w512 h774] [right x729 w512 h774] → leftShare 50.00% / rightShare 50.00%. box-shadow on BOTH plates: "color(srgb 0.11 0.098 0.09 / 0.5) 8px 8px 0px 0px" (picker card 512x684.8, About card 512x774). VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §3 law 1: exactly golden 61.8033989%/38.1966011% or preview-dominant 66.6666667%/33.3333333%, protagonist law 61.8–66.7%. §3 law 8: "Supporting fixtures do not compete with it through equal size or equal shadow." §3.1 line 58: "About is a quiet trailing destination rather than Picker's companion." PROPORTION-AUDIT PR-04 disposes "Empty/equal companion Cards" as REMOVE. Visible directly in docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/visual/shots/safari-desktop-light/picker.png.
```

**Reproduction.** At 1440x900 on /, read getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.pane-container')).gridTemplateColumns → "512px 512px", and getComputedStyle(el).boxShadow for both [data-slot=card] elements wider than 300px → identical strings.

**Proposed cure.** Retire About as Picker's companion per §3.1 (it is a trailing route, not an inspector) and transpose the picker to the producer InstrumentChassis with its exact golden stage/inspector ratio. If an inspector is genuinely absent, §3.1 is explicit that it "leaves no filler" — the stage takes the full width rather than being halved by a peer document. D-11 (the duplicated ColorSpaceSelector) dies with this.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The published @mkbabb/value.js/color surface withholds SPACE_SCHEMA, SPACE_IDS, makeColor and isAnyColor — all implemented and module-exported in src/color/model.ts — so the flagship consumer re-implements the whole abstraction in the demo, including a 17-case switch that is makeColor with type safety stripped.

**Mechanism.** Barrel under-export: the module layer implements the right abstraction, the public barrel withholds it, so every dynamic-space consumer must re-derive both the schema and the generic constructor.

**Evidence.**

```
src/color/model.ts:56 (SPACE_SCHEMA), :76 (SPACE_IDS), :127 (isAnyColor), :136 (makeColor). src/color/index.ts (41 lines) re-exports none. Pasted run: `node --input-type=module -e "await import('./dist/subpaths/color.js')"` -> published exports: a98Rgb, convertColor, displayP3, hsl, hsv, hwb, ictcp, interpolateHue, jzazbz, kelvin, lab, lch, linearSrgb, mapColorToGamut, mixColors, oklab, oklch, prophotoRgb, rec2020, rgb, safeAccentColor, toRgba8, xyz; makeColor -> ABSENT, SPACE_SCHEMA -> ABSENT, SPACE_IDS -> ABSENT, isAnyColor -> ABSENT. `grep -c 'makeColor|SPACE_SCHEMA' dist/subpaths/color.d.ts` = 0. Duplicates: demo/color-session/picker-color.ts:52-70 (PICKER_CHANNELS), :92-95 (CSS_PICKER_SPACES, byte-identical to src/css/grammar.ts:161-164), :123-144 (buildColor). Corroboration: across all of demo/picker/** the only library import is `clamp` from @mkbabb/value.js/math (4 sites); ColorPicker.vue imports zero value.js.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js && node --input-type=module -e "const m = await import('./dist/subpaths/color.js'); for (const w of ['makeColor','SPACE_SCHEMA','SPACE_IDS','isAnyColor']) console.log(w, w in m ? 'PRESENT' : 'ABSENT');"

**Proposed cure.** Publish the schema and the generic constructor: src/color/index.ts re-exports SPACE_SCHEMA, SPACE_IDS, makeColor, isAnyColor; src/subpaths/color.ts follows. Extend SPACE_SCHEMA channels to {key,min,max,unit,hue} (the library already privately owns those bounds — it clamps kelvin at model.ts:91 and gamut-maps against them). Then delete from the demo: buildColor, CSS_PICKER_SPACES, and the channel-name/hue columns of PICKER_CHANNELS; picker-color.ts collapses to a thin Result->throw adapter.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-2 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** App-scoped state (ActionBarContext + the colour-edit state machine) lives inside a KeepAlive'd route-leaf component and is reached by instance ref; the mobile mount path never captures that ref, so the picker's entire dock action bar is absent at mobile widths.

**Mechanism.** Ownership inversion — state whose lifetime must be the app's is assembled in a component whose lifetime is a pane-routing accident, then reached imperatively through a template ref captured on only one of two mount paths.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:315-328 builds actionBarContext; :331-344 defineExpose. App.vue:38 `:action-bar="colorPickerRef?.actionBarContext ?? null"`, :41-42 commit/cancel, :342 usePaneRouter, :351 usePaletteWiring. colorPickerRef is populated only by onDesktopLeftMount (App.vue:323-328), wired to the desktop PaneSlots at :105 and :131; the mobile PaneSlot at App.vue:83-91 passes no :on-mount, and PaneSlot.vue:124 renders `:ref="onMount ? ... : undefined"`. MEASURED live at http://localhost:9000/#/ : at 1440x900 nav exposes Back, Reset color, Copy color, Random color, Palettes, Extract palette, Open color input; at 390x844 (data-layout=mobile) it exposes NONE and the #action-bar DockLayer is absent from the DOM (actionBarLayerPresent:false), with the toggle surviving as a 32x32 button inside a 0-width .action-bar-toggle-slot at tabindex=-1. Corroborated by the shipped shots: safari-desktop-light/picker.png shows the 'Tools' group; safari-mobile-light/picker.png does not.
```

**Reproduction.** With the dev server on :9000, open http://localhost:9000/#/ at 1440x900 and evaluate `[...document.querySelectorAll('nav button')].map(b=>b.getAttribute('aria-label'))` -> includes 'Reset color','Copy color','Random color','Palettes','Extract palette','Open color input'. Resize to 390x844, reload http://localhost:9000/#/, evaluate the same -> none of those six; `!!document.querySelector('nav [id="action-bar"]')` -> false.

**Proposed cure.** Move ActionBarContext and the commit/cancel edit machine into a color-session composable (useColorEditSession / useActionBarContext) provided by App beside COLOR_MODEL_KEY; the Dock injects it. defineExpose and colorPickerRef disappear entirely, and with them usePaletteWiring's picker parameter and its retry loop.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-1 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The demo typechecks `@mkbabb/value.js/css` against the PUBLISHED npm 4.0.0 tarball while running it against the LOCAL working-tree build. `tsconfig.demo.json#paths` is a hand-copied mirror of `package.json#exports` and has drifted in both directions: 3 phantom entries (`.` → dist/index.d.ts, `/parsing`, `/units` — all three target files that do not exist) and 2 MISSING real subpaths (`/css`, `/value`). The subject component's composable straddles the seam on adjacent lines 3 and 4.

Missing targets verified: dist/index.d.ts MISSING, dist/subpaths/parsing.d.ts MISSING, dist/subpaths/units.d.ts MISSING. Blast radius: 9 demo files import /css.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js; printf 'import { convertColor } from "@mkbabb/value.js/color";\nimport { parseCssColor } from "@mkbabb/value.js/css";\nexport const x=[convertColor,parseCssColor];\n' > /tmp/probe.ts; printf '{"extends":"/Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js/tsconfig.demo.json","compilerOptions":{"noEmit":true,"traceResolution":true},"include":["probe.ts"]}' > /tmp/tsconfig.probe.json; npx tsc -p /tmp/tsconfig.probe.json 2>&1 | grep -E 'value\.js/(css|color)'

**Proposed cure.** Generate tsconfig.demo.json#paths from package.json#exports with the SAME generator vite.config.ts already uses for valueJsSelfAlias — one map, one generator, two consumers. Emit a tsconfig.value-paths.json that tsconfig.demo.json extends; add a golden that fails when any value.js subpath's traceResolution line carries a `Package ID`. Land FIRST: this is the only defect that makes the rest of the repo's evidence untrustworthy (the V·π parser mini-tranche edits src/css/, which the demo typecheck currently cannot see).
